const fs=require('fs'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const damage=[];
const c=vm.createContext({artilleryExplosionDuration:.9,drawArtilleryExplosion(){},damagePlayer:(player,amount)=>{damage.push(amount);return true;},getCombatHitbox:p=>({x:p.x,y:p.y,rx:8,ry:8})});
for(const n of ['game-config','boss-behaviors'])vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync('Development/'+n+'.js','utf8').replace(/^import .*;\r?\n/gm,'').replace('bossBehaviorSettings as settings','bossBehaviorSettings').replaceAll('export ',''),c);
vm.runInContext('var measuredNeruCenter=neruBodyCenterOffsetY;neruBodyCenterOffsetY=()=>0;var measuredBodyCenter=arisuBodyCenterOffsetY;arisuBodyCenterOffsetY=()=>0;var settings=bossBehaviorSettings;settings.arisu.warningOffsetY=0',c);
const run=s=>vm.runInContext(s,c);
run('var attackRoll=0;var ai=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>attackRoll});var player={x:500,y:300,contactIFrames:0};var view={x:0,y:0,width:1280,height:720};var arisu={id:"arisu",type:"arisu",x:200,y:300,spriteSize:200,ai:true,contactDamage:15,bossCooldown:0}');
assert.equal(run('ai.updateNpc(arisu,player,.1,view)'),true);
assert.equal(run('arisu.bossAction.clip'),'charge');
run('ai.updateNpc(arisu,player,2.8,view)');assert.equal(run('arisu.x'),200);
run('ai.updateNpc(arisu,player,.1,view)');assert.equal(run('arisu.bossAction.name'),'charge');
run('player.y=600;ai.updateNpc(arisu,player,.1,view)');assert.equal(run('arisu.y'),300);assert(run('arisu.x')>200);
run('player.y=340;var iroha={id:"iroha",type:"iroha",x:200,y:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:20};ai.updateNpc(iroha,player,1.75,view)');
assert.equal(run('iroha.attack.name'),'cannon');assert.equal(run('ai.projectileCount'),1);
run('ai.updateNpc(iroha,player,1,view);player.y=600;attackRoll=.99;iroha.bossCooldown=0;ai.updateNpc(iroha,player,.5,view)');
assert.equal(run('iroha.attack.name'),'artillery');assert.equal(run('ai.zoneCount'),5);
run('var neru={id:"neru",type:"neru",x:100,y:100,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:20};ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.1,view)');
assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.clip'),'shoot');assert(run('ai.projectileCount')>=13);
run('ai.updateNpc(neru,player,2,view);neru.bossCooldown=0;ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.1,view)');
assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.name'),'compress');assert(run('neru.bossSquash')<1);
run('ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.9,view)');assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.name'),'leap');
run('player.x+=100;ai.updateNpc(neru,player,1,view)');assert(run('neru.bossVisualOffsetY')<-720);
run('player.x+=100;ai.updateNpc(neru,player,2.9,view)');assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.aimLocked'),false);
run('ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.22,view);var targetX=neru.bossAction.targetX;player.x+=200;ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.9,view)');assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.targetX'),run('targetX'));assert(run('neru.bossVisualOffsetY')<-720);
run('ai.updateNpc(neru,player,.1,view);ai.updateNpc(neru,player,1,view)');assert.equal(run('neru.x'),run('targetX'));assert.equal(run('neru.bossVisualOffsetY'),0);assert.equal(run('neru.bossAction.name'),'recover');
assert.equal(run('ai.updateNpc({type:"shiroko",ai:true},player,1,view)'),false);
assert.equal(run('ai.updateNpc({...arisu,ai:false},player,1,view)'),false);
run('ai.updateEffects(0,player,[])');assert.equal(run('ai.projectileCount'),0);assert.equal(run('ai.zoneCount'),0);
console.log('PASS: Arisu 3s charge and locked direction; Iroha alternating cannon/artillery; Neru bullets/compress/leap; Shiroko chase fallback; dead-owner cleanup.');

// Projectile impacts share normal damage I-frames, and dead owners leave no attacks behind.
run('var effects=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var shooter={id:"tank",type:"iroha",x:200,y:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:20};player.x=500;player.y=300;player.contactIFrames=0;effects.updateNpc(shooter,player,1.75,view);player.y=330;effects.updateEffects(.7,player,[shooter])');
assert.equal(damage.at(-1),20);assert(run('player.contactIFrames')>0);assert.equal(run('effects.projectileCount'),1);
// Arisu warns without moving, draws converging lane lines, and sweeps for fixed 20 dash damage.
run('var dashAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var dashBoss={id:"dash",type:"arisu",x:100,y:300,spriteSize:200,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:15};player.x=500;player.y=300;player.contactIFrames=0;dashAI.updateNpc(dashBoss,player,1,view)');
const lines=[];c.warningContext={save(){},restore(){},translate(){},rotate(){},fillRect(){},strokeRect(){},beginPath(){},moveTo(x,y){lines.push(y)},lineTo(){},stroke(){}};
run('dashAI.draw(warningContext,{x:0,y:0})');assert(lines.some(v=>v>0&&v<97));assert.equal(run('dashBoss.x'),100);
run('dashAI.updateNpc(dashBoss,player,2,view);dashAI.updateNpc(dashBoss,player,1,view)');assert.equal(damage.at(-1),20);assert(run('player.contactIFrames')>0);
run('player.y=600;dashAI.updateNpc(dashBoss,player,.2,view)');assert.equal(run('dashBoss.y'),300);
console.log('PASS: translucent converging warning lane, stationary windup, locked dash and fixed20 damage.');

assert(run('dashBoss.bossAction.speed')>0);run('dashAI.updateNpc(dashBoss,player,1,view)');assert.equal(run('dashBoss.bossAction'),null);
run('var centered=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var centeredBoss={id:"centered",type:"arisu",x:100,y:300,spriteSize:240,spriteHurtbox:{offsetX:12,offsetY:-40},ai:true,bossCooldown:0};var translates=[];warningContext.translate=(x,y)=>translates.push([x,y]);centered.updateNpc(centeredBoss,player,1,view);centered.draw(warningContext,{x:0,y:0})');
assert.equal(run('translates[0][0]'),112);assert.equal(run('translates[0][1]'),300);
console.log('PASS: sprite-centered warning and deceleration after passing launch-time player position.');

run('settings.arisu.warningOffsetY=20;var lockAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var lockBoss={id:"lock",type:"arisu",x:100,y:300,spriteSize:240,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=500;player.y=320;lockAI.updateNpc(lockBoss,player,2,view);var lockedAngle=lockBoss.bossAction.angle;player.y=600;lockAI.updateNpc(lockBoss,player,.9,view)');
assert.equal(run('lockBoss.bossAction.angle'),run('lockedAngle'));assert.equal(run('lockBoss.bossAction.name'),'chargeUp');
run('lockAI.updateNpc(lockBoss,player,.1,view)');assert.equal(run('lockBoss.bossAction.name'),'charge');assert.equal(run('lockBoss.bossAction.angle'),run('lockedAngle'));
console.log('PASS: three-second windup with aim frozen for the final second.');

// Timed braking is smooth and frame-step independent in every travel direction.
for(const [dx,dy] of [[400,300],[-400,300],[400,-300],[-400,-300],[400,0],[0,-300]]) {
 c.testDX=dx;c.testDY=dy;
 run('settings.arisu.warningOffsetY=0;var axisAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var axisBoss={id:"axis",type:"arisu",x:600,y:400,spriteSize:240,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=600+testDX;player.y=400+testDY;axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,2,view);axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,1,view);var initialSpeed=axisBoss.bossAction.speed;axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,axisBoss.bossAction.brakeAt/1375,view);axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,.75,view)');
 assert(run('axisBoss.bossAction.speed')>0);assert(run('axisBoss.bossAction.speed')<run('initialSpeed')/2);
 run('axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,.74,view)');assert(run('axisBoss.bossAction.speed')>0);
 run('axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,.01,view)');assert.equal(run('axisBoss.bossAction'),null);
 assert.equal(run('axisAI.updateNpc(axisBoss,player,.1,view)'),false);assert(run('axisBoss.bossCooldown')>7);
}
console.log('PASS: continuous 1.5-second braking and chase fallback in cardinal and diagonal directions.');

run('var stopAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var stopBoss={id:"stop",type:"arisu",x:200,y:300,spriteSize:240,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=600;player.y=300;stopAI.updateNpc(stopBoss,player,2,view);stopAI.updateNpc(stopBoss,player,1,view);for(let i=0;i<240;i++)stopAI.updateNpc(stopBoss,player,1/120,view)');
assert.equal(run('stopBoss.bossAction'),null);assert(Math.abs(run('stopBoss.x')-820)<1e-6);assert.equal(run('stopAI.updateNpc(stopBoss,player,.1,view)'),false);
console.log('PASS: full-speed launch, 1.5s braking, and no repeated charging.');
assert(run('measuredBodyCenter({spriteSize:446.208})')>60);assert.equal(run('initialSpeed'),1375);

// Reproduce nearly horizontal leftward flight with the actual body-center offset.
run('arisuBodyCenterOffsetY=measuredBodyCenter;var runaway=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var runner={id:"runaway",type:"arisu",x:900,y:300,spriteSize:446.208,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=600;player.y=300+measuredBodyCenter(runner)-.01;runaway.updateNpc(runner,player,2,view);player.x=350;runaway.updateNpc(runner,player,1,view);var distance=runner.bossAction.targetDistance;var lastOwned=true;for(let i=0;i<240&&runner.bossAction;i++)lastOwned=runaway.updateNpc(runner,player,1/120,view)');
assert(run('distance')<551);assert(run('runner.x')>129);assert.equal(run('runner.bossAction'),null);assert.equal(run('lastOwned'),false);assert.equal(run('runner.gifElapsed'),0);assert(run('runner.bossCooldown')>7);
console.log('PASS: near-horizontal left dash has bounded range and hands back normal chase immediately.');

// A player fleeing left must be passed before braking starts; aim itself stays locked.
run('var fleeAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var fleeBoss={id:"flee",type:"arisu",x:1000,y:300,spriteSize:446.208,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=700;player.y=300+measuredBodyCenter(fleeBoss);fleeAI.updateNpc(fleeBoss,player,2,view);player.x=460;fleeAI.updateNpc(fleeBoss,player,1,view);var fleeAngle=fleeBoss.bossAction.angle;var passed=false;for(let i=0;i<240&&fleeBoss.bossAction;i++){player.x-=240/120;fleeAI.updateNpc(fleeBoss,player,1/120,view);if(fleeBoss.bossAction?.brakingElapsed>0&&!passed){passed=true;if(fleeBoss.x>player.x+1e-6)throw Error("Braking before passing fleeing player");}if(fleeBoss.bossAction&&fleeBoss.bossAction.angle!==fleeAngle)throw Error("Dash re-aimed")}');
assert(run('passed'));assert.equal(run('fleeBoss.bossAction'),null);
console.log('PASS: fleeing left player is crossed before braking, without changing locked dash direction.');
for(const side of [-1,1]) {
 c.side=side;
 run('var cannonAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var tank={id:"warning-tank",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:20};player.x=600+side*200;player.y=340;cannonAI.updateNpc(tank,player,.5,view);var angles=[];warningContext.rotate=a=>angles.push(a);cannonAI.updateEffects(0,player,[tank]);cannonAI.draw(warningContext,{x:0,y:0})');
 assert.equal(run('cannonAI.projectileCount'),0);assert.equal(run('tank.x'),600);assert.equal(run('tank.y'),300);assert.equal(run('tank.moving'),false);
 assert.equal(run('angles[0]'),side<0?Math.PI:0);
 run('player.x=600-side*200;cannonAI.updateNpc(tank,player,1.24,view)');assert.equal(run('cannonAI.projectileCount'),0);
 run('cannonAI.updateNpc(tank,player,.01,view)');assert.equal(run('cannonAI.projectileCount'),1);
 // Player crossed sides during windup: the shot must retain the warned horizontal direction.
 run('player.x=600+side*400;player.y=330;player.contactIFrames=0;cannonAI.updateEffects(.5,player,[tank])');assert.equal(damage.at(-1),20);
}
console.log('PASS: stationary 1s cannon warning, left/right lock, persistent warning and horizontal shell damage.');

const plasmaCalls=[];c.drawLuminousPlasma=(...args)=>plasmaCalls.push(args);
run('var visualAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var visualTank={id:"visual",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:25};player.x=900;player.y=340;visualAI.updateNpc(visualTank,player,1.75,view);visualAI.draw(warningContext,{x:0,y:0})');
assert.equal(plasmaCalls.length,1);assert.equal(plasmaCalls[0][2].speed,2400);assert.equal(plasmaCalls[0][2].color,'orange');assert.equal(plasmaCalls[0][3],20);
console.log('PASS: cannon uses shared orange plasma bloom/tapered-tail renderer at2400px/s.');
// Warning reaches back into the tank; projectile bloom width and collision diameter match72px lane.
run('var backAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var backTank={id:"back",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=900;var laneRects=[];warningContext.fillRect=(...r)=>laneRects.push(r);backAI.updateNpc(backTank,player,.5,view);backAI.draw(warningContext,{x:0,y:0})');
assert(run('laneRects[0][0]')<0);assert.equal(run('laneRects[0][3]'),72);
assert.equal(plasmaCalls[0][3]*3.6,72);
console.log('PASS: warning extends behind muzzle into tank; visible bloom diameter matches72px lane.');
run('var chooser={x:600,y:300};player.x=900;player.y=340');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>0)'), 'cannon');
run('player.x=600;player.y=100');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>.99)'), 'artillery');
run('player.x=900;player.y=200');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{x:0,y:24},()=>.99)'), 'artillery');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{x:0,y:-24},()=>.99)'), 'artillery');
run('var delayed=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});var delayedTank={id:"delayed",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=900;player.y=340;delayed.updateNpc(delayedTank,player,1,view)');assert.equal(run('delayed.projectileCount'),0);
run('delayed.updateNpc(delayedTank,player,.74,view)');assert.equal(run('delayed.projectileCount'),0);
run('delayed.updateNpc(delayedTank,player,.01,view)');assert.equal(run('delayed.projectileCount'),1);
console.log('PASS: shell waits1.75s; horizontal alignment/entry favors cannon, vertical alignment or moving away selects artillery.');

assert(Math.abs(run('delayedTank.attack.elapsed')-220)<1e-6);
assert.equal(run('Math.floor((delayedTank.attack.elapsed+1e-6)/110)'),2);
console.log('PASS: shell release synchronized to cannon muzzle-flash frame2 at1.75seconds.');

run('player.x=900;player.y=300');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChances(chooser,player,.1).cannon'),1);assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>.999999)'), 'cannon');
run('player.x=600;player.y=100');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChances(chooser,player,.1).artillery'),1);
run('player.x=900;player.y=320;var chance=irohaAttackChances(chooser,player,.1).cannon');assert(run('chance')>0&&run('chance')<1);
assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>0)'), 'cannon');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>.99999999)'), 'artillery');
assert.equal(run('effects.projectileCount'),1);
console.log('PASS: weighted cast-time pool, equalY guarantees cannon, equalX favors artillery and cannon persists after hitting player.');
// A piercing shell damages the player only once even if they overlap it again after I-frames expire.
const beforeRepeat=damage.length;
run('player.contactIFrames=0;player.x=1974;player.y=340;effects.updateEffects(.01,player,[shooter])');
assert.equal(damage.length,beforeRepeat);assert.equal(run('effects.projectileCount'),1);
console.log('PASS: piercing shell cannot repeatedly damage the same player.');
// Intercept unchanged trajectories on either side, including diagonal entry after a delayed release.
for(const scenario of [{x:900,y:340,vx:240,vy:0},{x:300,y:340,vx:-240,vy:0},{x:1200,y:52,vx:-80,vy:96}]) {
 c.scenario=scenario;
 run('var interceptTank={id:"intercept",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:25};player.x=scenario.x;player.y=scenario.y;player.contactIFrames=0;var motion={x:scenario.vx*.01,y:scenario.vy*.01};var plan=irohaCannonIntercept(interceptTank,player,.01,motion)');
 assert(run('plan')!==null);
 const countBefore=damage.length;
 run('var interceptAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>0});for(let i=0;i<500;i++){player.x+=motion.x;player.y+=motion.y;interceptAI.updateNpc(interceptTank,player,.01,view,motion);interceptAI.updateEffects(.01,player,[interceptTank]);}');
 assert.equal(damage.length,countBefore+1);
 if(scenario.vy)assert(run('plan.fireAt')>1.75);
}
run('player.x=900;player.y=52');assert.equal(run('irohaCannonIntercept(interceptTank,player,.01,{x:0,y:-.96})'),null);
console.log('PASS: unchanged horizontal/diagonal trajectories are hit with timed shell interception; unreachable trajectories rejected.');

// Axis-based pool interpolates by angle in every quadrant.
for(const [dx,dy,wanted] of [[300,0,1],[-300,0,1],[0,300,0],[0,-300,0],[300,300,.5],[-300,300,.5],[300,-300,.5],[-300,-300,.5]]) {
 c.axisX=dx;c.axisY=dy;
 run('player.x=chooser.x+axisX;player.y=chooser.y+axisY');
 assert(Math.abs(run('irohaAttackChances(chooser,player).cannon')-wanted)<1e-10);
}
run('player.x=900;player.y=600');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>.49)'), 'cannon');assert.equal(run('irohaAttackChoice(chooser,player,.1,{},()=>.5)'), 'artillery');
console.log('PASS: horizontal100% cannon, vertical100% artillery, diagonal50/50 across all quadrants.');

const angleChance=degrees=>{c.degrees=degrees;return run('irohaAttackChances({x:0,y:0},{x:Math.cos(degrees*Math.PI/180)*100,y:Math.sin(degrees*Math.PI/180)*100}).cannon')};
assert(Math.abs(angleChance(45)-.5)<1e-10);
assert(Math.abs(angleChance(10)-.9)<1e-10);
assert(angleChance(30)>.5&&angleChance(30)<.9);
assert(Math.abs(angleChance(80)-.1)<1e-10);
assert(angleChance(30)-angleChance(45)>angleChance(0)-angleChance(15));
assert(Math.abs(angleChance(30)+angleChance(60)-1)<1e-10);
let previous=1;for(let degrees=0;degrees<=90;degrees++){const value=angleChance(degrees);assert(value<=previous+1e-10&&value>=0&&value<=1);previous=value;}
console.log('PASS: quadrant sigmoid100% cannon at0deg, high but noncertain at10deg, gentle transition with10deg90percent,45deg50/50 and90deg100% artillery.');

for(const roll of [0,.999999]) {
 c.attackRollTest=roll;
 run('var mobileAI=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>attackRollTest});var mobileTank={id:"mobile",type:"iroha",x:600,y:300,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=900;player.y=320;var chaseSteps=0;var moveTank=n=>{n.x+=5;n.moving=true;chaseSteps++};mobileAI.updateNpc(mobileTank,player,.1,view,{},moveTank);var moveName=mobileTank.bossAction.name;mobileAI.updateNpc(mobileTank,player,.1,view,{},moveTank)');
 assert.equal(run('chaseSteps'),2);assert.equal(run('mobileTank.x'),610);assert.equal(run('mobileTank.moving'),true);
 assert.equal(run('mobileTank.attack.name'),roll===0?'cannon':'artillery');
}
console.log('PASS: Iroha continues chase during both cannon and artillery without replacing attack pose.');
const plus=run('irohaArtilleryPoints({x:0,y:0},{x:300,y:200},{x:5,y:0})');assert.equal(plus.length,5);assert(Math.abs(plus[1].y-200)<1e-8);
const cross=run('irohaArtilleryPoints({x:0,y:0},{x:300,y:200},{x:5,y:5})');assert(cross[1].x>300&&cross[1].y>200);
run('var rain=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>.999999});var rainTank={id:"rain",type:"iroha",x:600,y:100,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0,contactDamage:25};player.x=600;player.y=340;player.contactIFrames=0;rain.updateNpc(rainTank,player,.4,view)');assert.equal(run('rain.zoneCount'),5);
const rainBefore=damage.length;
run('rain.updateEffects(1.49,player,[rainTank])');assert.equal(damage.length,rainBefore);
run('rain.updateEffects(.01,player,[rainTank])');assert.equal(damage.length,rainBefore);
run('rain.updateEffects(.34,player,[rainTank])');assert.equal(damage.length,rainBefore);
run('rain.updateEffects(.011,player,[rainTank])');assert.equal(damage.length,rainBefore+1);assert.equal(damage.at(-1),25);assert.equal(run('rain.zoneCount'),5);
run('rain.updateEffects(6,player,[rainTank])');assert.equal(run('rain.zoneCount'),0);
console.log('PASS: +/X patterns,1.5s warning, falling-shell delay, impact-only damage and explosion lifetime cleanup.');

for(const motion of [{x:5,y:0},{x:5,y:5}]) {
 c.previewMotion=motion;
 const pts=run('irohaArtilleryPoints({x:0,y:0},{x:640,y:360},previewMotion,{width:1280,height:720})');
 const spanX=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.x))-Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.x))+pts[0].radius*2;
 const spanY=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.y))-Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.y))+pts[0].radius*2;
 assert(Math.abs(spanX-spanY)<1e-8);
 assert(spanX<=720*1.4+1e-8);
 for(const p of pts.slice(1))assert(Math.abs(Math.hypot(p.x-640,p.y-360)-pts[0].radius*1.8)<1e-8);
 assert.equal(pts.length,5);
}
run('var animatedRain=createBossBehaviors({random:()=>.999999});var animationTank={id:"animated-rain",type:"iroha",x:600,y:100,spriteSize:300,ai:true,bossCooldown:0};player.x=600;player.y=340;animatedRain.updateNpc(animationTank,player,.4,view);var radii=[];var circleContext={save(){},restore(){},beginPath(){},arc(x,y,r){radii.push(r)},fill(){},stroke(){}};animatedRain.draw(circleContext,{x:0,y:0})');
const initialInner=run('radii[1]');run('radii=[];animatedRain.updateEffects(.75,player,[animationTank]);animatedRain.draw(circleContext,{x:0,y:0})');assert(run('radii[1]')<initialInner);assert.equal(run('radii[0]'),180);
console.log('PASS: compact equal-circle +/X clusters with slight overlap and animated closing rings.');

const beforeFalling=plasmaCalls.length;
run('animatedRain.updateEffects(.85,player,[animationTank]);animatedRain.draw(circleContext,{x:0,y:0})');

function element(tag){return {tag,children:[],hidden:false,dataset:{},attrs:{},append(...nodes){for(const n of nodes){n.parent=this;this.children.push(n)}},remove(){this.parent.children=this.parent.children.filter(n=>n!==this)},setAttribute(k,v){this.attrs[k]=v},getContext(){return {clearRect(){},save(){},restore(){},translate(){},rotate(){},scale(){},drawImage(){}}}};}
const c=vm.createContext({document:{createElement:element},Image:class {constructor(){images++;this.naturalWidth=100;this.naturalHeight=100;}set src(value){this.onload?.()}},drawSynergyIcon:()=>false,supportIcon:id=>`<svg>${id}</svg>`,createBitmapText:canvas=>({setText(text){canvas.text=text}})});
vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync('Development/buff-hud.js','utf8').replace(/^import .*;\r?\n/gm,'').replaceAll('export ',''),c);const run=s=>vm.runInContext(s,c);c.root=element('div');run("var hud=createBuffHud(root),p={hp:70,characterSkills:[{id:'phrenapates',name:'Phrenapates',icon:'skill.png'}],phrenapatesState:{stacks:4},combatBuffs:{phrenapates:{critPercent:8}},attackSlots:[],equipmentSlots:[]};hud.update(p)");
assert.equal(c.root.children.length,1);const icon=c.root.children[0],counter=icon.children[2];assert.equal(counter.text,'4');assert.equal(icon.attrs['aria-label'],'Phrenapates, 4 stacks');assert.equal(counter.hidden,false);assert.equal(images,1);

run("p.attackSlots=[{id:'luminous-nova',name:'Luminous Nova',icon:'weapon.png',skillCooldownRemaining:20}];p.combatBuffs['luminous-nova-ex']={critPercent:22.3};hud.update(p)");assert.equal(c.root.children.length,2);assert.equal(c.root.children[1].children[2].hidden,true);const loaded=images;run('p.attackSlots[0].skillCooldownRemaining=19;hud.update(p)');assert.equal(images,loaded,'Cooldown changes must not reload artwork');
run("delete p.combatBuffs.phrenapates;hud.update(p)");assert.equal(c.root.children.length,1);assert(!c.root.children.includes(icon));run("p.combatBuffs={};hud.update(p)");assert.equal(c.root.children.length,0);assert.equal(c.root.hidden,true);
run("p.equipmentSlots=[{id:'sports-drink',name:'Sports Drink',icon:'drink.png',level:1}];p.sportsDrinkState={crit:2.5,foodRemaining:4};hud.update(p)");assert.equal(c.root.children.length,2);assert.equal(c.root.children[0].children[2].text,'2.5');assert.equal(c.root.children[1].children[2].hidden,true);

run("p.combatBuffs.future={name:'Future Buff',icon:'future.png',stacks:3};p.supportState={shieldRemaining:5};hud.update(p)");assert.equal(c.root.children.length,2);assert.equal(c.root.children[0].children[2].text,'3');assert.equal(c.root.children[1].children[1].hidden,false);
run('p.hp=0;hud.update(p)');assert.equal(c.root.children.length,0);run('p={hp:140,label:"Arona"};hud.update(p)');assert.equal(c.root.hidden,true);

// Homing launch positions follow the target direction, not the player's aim-facing muzzle.
const playerBody=run('getCombatHitbox(p,false)');
for(const [dx,dy] of [[-1,0],[-1,-1],[-1,1],[1,-1],[1,1]]){
  enemies.clear();const length=Math.hypot(dx,dy),ux=dx/length,uy=dy/length;
  enemies.set('muzzle-target',{id:'muzzle-target',type:'mob',x:playerBody.x+ux*300,y:playerBody.y+uy*300,hp:10000,spriteSize:20});
  run('var muzzleAttack=createDecagrammatonAttack({random:()=>.5});drawn=[];muzzleAttack.update(.000001,p,0,{x:-1000,y:-1000,width:2000,height:2000});muzzleAttack.draw({},{});');
  const blue=run('drawn.find(b=>b.color==="blue")');assert(blue);
  const spawnX=blue.x-Math.cos(blue.angle)*blue.speed*blue.age-playerBody.x;
  const spawnY=blue.y-Math.sin(blue.angle)*blue.speed*blue.age-playerBody.y;
  const expected=1/Math.hypot(ux/playerBody.rx,uy/playerBody.ry)+run('luminousNovaSettings.muzzleGap')+blue.diameter/2*run('luminousNovaSettings.bloomRadiusScale');
  assert(Math.abs(spawnX-ux*expected)<1e-5,`blue muzzle X must face target ${dx},${dy}`);
  assert(Math.abs(spawnY-uy*expected)<1e-5,`blue muzzle Y must face target ${dx},${dy}`);
  run('drawn=[];muzzleAttack.update(.02,p,0,{x:-1000,y:-1000,width:2000,height:2000});muzzleAttack.draw({},{});');
  const pink=run('drawn.find(b=>b.color==="pink")');assert(pink);
  assert.equal(pink.angle,0,'Pink shots retain the player aim');
  assert(pink.x>playerBody.x,'Pink muzzle stays on player-aim side even when blue target is behind');
  assert(Math.abs(pink.y-playerBody.y)<1e-8);
}
enemies.clear();run('muzzleAttack=createDecagrammatonAttack({random:()=>.5});drawn=[];muzzleAttack.update(.000001,p,Math.PI);muzzleAttack.draw({},{});');

console.log('PASS homing blue muzzle faces rear/all-quadrant targets with bloom clearance; pink and no-target fallback retain player aim.');

  assert(sides.every(s=>['right','top','bottom'].includes(s)));
  c.side=sides[0];run('router.record(side)');
  if(sides[0]==='right')run(`log.push(${frame/60})`);

run("strip.record('right')");
assert.deepEqual(Array.from(run("strip.rank(['left','right'],{x:4,y:0})")),['left']);
assert.deepEqual(Array.from(run("strip.rank(['right'],{x:4,y:0})")),[]);

--- SNAPSHOT FILE: Development/tools/build-font2-metrics.py | sha256=1e0abde29991981cfb42307e7b0784dd1d884a73f42d3613b4db2ab8b99ba398 | encoding=utf-8-sig ---
"""Refresh tight font2 glyph bounds using the shared20px character-cell layout."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
from PIL import Image
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
source=(root/'Development/font-metrics.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
original=json.loads(source[source.index('{'):source.rindex('}')+1])
image=Image.open(root/'sprites/font2.png').convert('RGBA')
assert image.size==(300,160),'Font2 layout changed; measure the new layout before rebuilding.'
metrics={}
for letter,glyph in original.items():
    x0=int((glyph['x']+glyph['width']/2)//20)*20
    y0=int((glyph['y']+glyph['height']/2)//20)*20
    bounds=image.crop((x0,y0,x0+20,y0+20)).getbbox()
    if bounds:
        x,y,right,bottom=bounds
        metrics[letter]={'x':x0+x,'y':y0+y,'width':right-x,'height':bottom-y,'offsetY':y}
    else: metrics[letter]=glyph
(root/'Development/font2-metrics.js').write_text('// Generated from updated font2.png; rebuild with tools/build-font2-metrics.py.\nexport const font2Metrics = '+json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+';\n',encoding='utf-8')
print('Refreshed font2 glyph measurements.')

--- SNAPSHOT FILE: Development/tools/build-thin-font.py | sha256=8edd92ecefa29512ef1b63da1191c6f1651034a47be60d250c649e0a397c9fc7 | encoding=utf-8-sig ---
"""Build a thin bitmap font without moving glyph cells or modifying the original sheet."""
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
source = Image.open(root / 'sprites/font.png').convert('RGBA')
pixels = np.array(source)
ink = ((pixels[:, :, :3].min(axis=2) > 180) & (pixels[:, :, 3] > 128)).astype(np.uint8)
# Zhang-Suen thinning preserves connected strokes and endpoints, unlike erosion.
while True:
    changed = False
    for phase in range(2):
        p = np.pad(ink, 1)
        n = [p[:-2,1:-1],p[:-2,2:],p[1:-1,2:],p[2:,2:],p[2:,1:-1],p[2:,:-2],p[1:-1,:-2],p[:-2,:-2]]
        count = sum(n)
        turns = sum(((n[i] == 0) & (n[(i+1)%8] == 1)).astype(np.uint8) for i in range(8))
        guard = (n[0]*n[2]*n[4] == 0) & (n[2]*n[4]*n[6] == 0) if phase == 0 else (n[0]*n[2]*n[6] == 0) & (n[0]*n[4]*n[6] == 0)
        remove = (ink == 1) & (count >= 2) & (count <= 6) & (turns == 1) & guard
        if remove.any(): ink[remove] = 0; changed = True
    if not changed: break
mask = ink.astype(bool)
pad = np.pad(mask, 1)
outline = np.zeros_like(mask)
for y in range(3):
    for x in range(3): outline |= pad[y:y+mask.shape[0],x:x+mask.shape[1]]
# Retain source alpha bounds so neighboring cells cannot bleed into each other.
outline &= pixels[:,:,3] > 0
result = np.zeros_like(pixels)
result[outline] = [43, 38, 59, 255]
result[mask] = [255, 255, 255, 255]
Image.fromarray(result).save(root / 'sprites/font-thin.png')
print('Generated font-thin.png, original dimensions/cells preserved.')

# Tighten advances around the thinner glyphs rather than retaining heavy-outline spacing.
import json
metrics_source = (root / 'Development/font-metrics.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
metrics = json.loads(metrics_source[metrics_source.index('{'):metrics_source.rindex('}')+1])
thin_image = Image.fromarray(result)
for glyph in metrics.values():
    bounds = thin_image.crop((glyph['x'], glyph['y'], glyph['x']+glyph['width'], glyph['y']+glyph['height'])).getbbox()
    if bounds:
        left, top, right, bottom = bounds
        glyph['x'] += left; glyph['y'] += top
        glyph['width'] = right-left; glyph['height'] = bottom-top; glyph['offsetY'] += top
(root / 'Development/font-thin-metrics.js').write_text('// Generated thin glyph bounds; rebuild with tools/build-thin-font.py.\nexport const thinFontMetrics = '+json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+';\n',encoding='utf-8')

<!doctype html><style>body{margin:0;background:#263950}canvas{display:block}</style><canvas width="800" height="640"></canvas><script>const studentHandbookSettings={damage:1.4,interval:6,minimumInterval:5,hitLimit:7,hitCooldown:20/60,area:1,duration:2,speed:3*60,orbitRadius:90,hitRadius:Math.hypot(40,45.6)/2,width:40,height:45.6,emergeSeconds:.35,returnSeconds:.35,trailCount:12,trailInterval:.04,trailDuration:.6,knockbackSeconds:5/60,knockbackSpeed:2*60,maxLevel:7};
function studentHandbookLevelStats(level=1){level=Math.max(1,Math.min(7,level));return {count:3+Number(level>=2)+Number(level>=4)+Number(level>=6),duration:2+Number(level>=3),damageBonuses:[...(level>=3?[30]:[]),...(level>=5?[40]:[]),...(level>=7?[40]:[])]};}
const strictRulesSettings={duration:10,ringScales:[1,1.5,2],orbitScales:[1,1.75,2.5]};const weaponSizeMultiplier=()=>1,startingWeaponProjectileCount=(p,id,count)=>count,getCombatHitbox=p=>({x:p.x,y:p.y,rx:10,ry:10}),getAllEnemies=()=>[],getEnemyHurtbox=e=>e,createEnemySpatialIndex=()=>({querySegment:()=>[]});
/** Books grow out from the player and shrink back into the player within their lifetime. */
function studentHandbookPose(age,duration,retiringAt=null,retiringScale=1){
 const smooth=t=>{const p=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));return p*p*(3-2*p);};
 const scale=retiringAt===null?Math.min(smooth(age/studentHandbookSettings.emergeSeconds),smooth((duration-age)/studentHandbookSettings.returnSeconds)):
  retiringScale*(1-smooth((age-retiringAt)/studentHandbookSettings.returnSeconds));
 return {scale,alpha:scale};
}
/** Books materialize equally spaced around the player; each owns its hit budget/cooldown. */
function createStudentHandbookAttack({random=Math.random}={}){
 const s=studentHandbookSettings;let owner=null,enabled=true,cooldown=0,time=0,books=[],fadingTrails=[],shots=0,image=null,exRemaining=0;
 const ready=typeof Image==='undefined'?Promise.resolve():new Promise(resolve=>{image=new Image();image.onload=resolve;image.onerror=()=>{image=null;resolve();};image.src=new URL('../sprites/attacks/Arius Student Handbook.png',location.href).href;});
 function reset(){owner=null;cooldown=0;time=0;books=[];fadingTrails=[];shots=0;exRemaining=0;}
 function fire(player,weapon,angle,ringScale=null,phaseOffset=0){
  const stats=studentHandbookLevelStats(weapon.level),body=getCombatHitbox(player,false),baseArea=weaponSizeMultiplier(player),orbitScale=ringScale===null?1:strictRulesSettings.orbitScales[strictRulesSettings.ringScales.indexOf(ringScale)],area=baseArea*(ringScale??1),radius=s.orbitRadius*baseArea*orbitScale;
  const count=startingWeaponProjectileCount(player,'student-handbook',stats.count);
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const phase=angle+phaseOffset+i*Math.PI*2/count;books.push({exSkill:ringScale!==null,damageMultiplier:ringScale??1,knockbackMultiplier:ringScale??1,speed:s.speed*orbitScale,x:body.x,y:body.y,phase,angle:phase,age:0,retiringAt:null,retiringScale:1,trail:[],trailElapsed:0,area,radius,count,duration:ringScale===null?stats.duration:strictRulesSettings.duration,damageBonuses:stats.damageBonuses,hitsLeft:ringScale===null?s.hitLimit:Infinity,hits:new Map()});shots++;}
 }
 function ageTrail(trail,delta){for(const ghost of trail)ghost.age+=delta;while(trail.length&&trail[0].age>=s.trailDuration)trail.shift();}
 function finish(index){const b=books[index];if(b.trail.length)fadingTrails.push({area:b.area,trail:b.trail});books.splice(index,1);}
 function cast(player,angle=0){
  const weapon=player.attackSlots?.find(w=>w?.id==='student-handbook');if(!weapon)return false;
  if(owner!==player){reset();owner=player;}while(books.length)finish(books.length-1);
  exRemaining=strictRulesSettings.duration;const count=startingWeaponProjectileCount(player,'student-handbook',studentHandbookLevelStats(weapon.level).count);
  strictRulesSettings.ringScales.forEach((scale,index)=>fire(player,weapon,angle,scale,index*Math.PI/count));return true;
 }
 function update(delta,player,angle=0){
  const weapon=player.attackSlots?.find(w=>w?.id==='student-handbook');if(!weapon){reset();return;}
  if(owner!==player){reset();owner=player;}if(!(delta>0))return;
  let left=delta,index=null;
  while(left>1e-8){
   if(enabled&&exRemaining<=1e-8&&cooldown<=1e-8){fire(player,weapon,angle);cooldown=Math.max(s.minimumInterval,attackTimeFormula(s.interval*(player.tuningParts?stationaryWeaponIntervalScale(player):1),ensureCombatStats(player).hastePercent));}
   const dt=Math.min(left,1/120,enabled&&cooldown>1e-8?cooldown:1/120);left-=dt;time+=dt;cooldown=Math.max(0,cooldown-dt);exRemaining=Math.max(0,exRemaining-dt);
   for(let i=fadingTrails.length-1;i>=0;i--){ageTrail(fadingTrails[i].trail,dt);if(!fadingTrails[i].trail.length)fadingTrails.splice(i,1);}
   if(!books.length)continue;if(!index)index=createEnemySpatialIndex(getAllEnemies(),getEnemyHurtbox);
   const center=getCombatHitbox(player,false);
   for(let i=books.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=books[i],from={x:b.x,y:b.y};b.age+=dt;
    const pose=studentHandbookPose(b.age,b.duration,b.retiringAt,b.retiringScale);
    if((b.retiringAt===null&&b.age>=b.duration-1e-8)||(b.retiringAt!==null&&b.age-b.retiringAt>=s.returnSeconds-1e-8)){finish(i);continue;}
    if(b.retiringAt===null)b.angle=b.phase+b.speed/b.radius*b.age;
    b.x=center.x+Math.cos(b.angle)*b.radius*pose.scale;b.y=center.y+Math.sin(b.angle)*b.radius*pose.scale;
    ageTrail(b.trail,dt);b.trailElapsed+=dt;
    if(b.trailElapsed>=s.trailInterval-1e-8){b.trailElapsed%=s.trailInterval;b.trail.push({x:b.x,y:b.y,angle:Math.sin(b.angle)*.12,scale:pose.scale,age:0});if(b.trail.length>s.trailCount)b.trail.shift();}
    if(b.retiringAt!==null||pose.scale<=0)continue;
    const radius=s.hitRadius*b.area*pose.scale;
    const candidates=index.querySegment(from,b,radius).filter(enemy=>{if(!getEnemy(enemy.id)||time-(b.hits.get(enemy.id)??-Infinity)<s.hitCooldown-1e-8)return false;const body=getEnemyHurtbox(enemy);return segmentHitsBody({...body,rx:body.rx+radius,ry:body.ry+radius},from.x,from.y,b.x,b.y);});
    for(const enemy of candidates){
     if(!getEnemy(enemy.id))continue;b.hits.set(enemy.id,time);
     const hit=rollAttackDamage(player,totalWeaponDamage(player,'student-handbook',s.damage,b.damageBonuses,{projectileCount:b.count,hitCooldownFrames:s.hitCooldown*60}),random,{target:enemy,weaponId:'student-handbook',damageMod:b.damageMultiplier,skillAttack:b.exSkill});
     if(damageEnemy(enemy.id,hit.damage,player,hit.type,{canMiss:true,weaponId:'student-handbook'})===false)continue;
     b.hitsLeft--;const target=getEnemy(enemy.id);
     if(target){const body=getEnemyHurtbox(target),direction=Math.atan2(body.y-center.y,body.x-center.x),distance=s.knockbackSpeed*s.knockbackSeconds*weaponKnockbackMultiplier(player)*b.knockbackMultiplier;
      const motion=beginKnockback(target,Math.cos(direction)*distance,Math.sin(direction)*distance,s.knockbackSeconds);
      if(motion){target.skillKnockback=motion;target.knockbackMovementRemaining=Math.max(target.knockbackMovementRemaining||0,s.knockbackSeconds);}
     }
     if(b.hitsLeft<=0)break;
    }
    if(b.hitsLeft<=0){b.retiringAt=b.age;b.retiringScale=pose.scale;}
   }
  }
 }
 function drawPose(context,camera,b,pose,alpha,shrink=1){
  const scale=b.area*pose.scale*shrink;if(scale<=0)return;
  context.save();context.globalAlpha=alpha;context.translate(pose.x-camera.x,pose.y-camera.y);context.rotate(pose.angle);context.scale(scale,scale);context.drawImage(image,48,12,200,228,-s.width/2,-s.height/2,s.width,s.height);context.restore();
 }
 function drawTrail(context,camera,b){for(const ghost of b.trail){const fade=Math.max(0,1-ghost.age/s.trailDuration);drawPose(context,camera,b,ghost,.25*fade*fade,fade);}}
 function draw(context,camera){
  if(!image?.complete||!image.naturalWidth)return;
  for(const b of fadingTrails)drawTrail(context,camera,b);
  for(const b of books){drawTrail(context,camera,b);const pose=studentHandbookPose(b.age,b.duration,b.retiringAt,b.retiringScale);drawPose(context,camera,b,{x:b.x,y:b.y,angle:Math.sin(b.angle)*.12,scale:pose.scale},pose.alpha);}
 }
 return {ready,cast,update,draw,reset,get exRemaining(){return exRemaining;},setAutoattackEnabled(value){enabled=Boolean(value);},get shotsFired(){return shots;},get projectileCount(){return books.filter(b=>b.retiringAt===null).length;}};
}
const a=createStudentHandbookAttack(),p={x:400,y:320,attackSlots:[{id:'student-handbook',level:7}]};a.setAutoattackEnabled(false);a.ready.then(()=>{a.cast(p,0);a.update(.4,p,0);a.draw(document.querySelector('canvas').getContext('2d'),{x:0,y:0});});</script>

--- SNAPSHOT FILE: outputs/iroha-chance-curve/plot.py | sha256=d39132ffe3e80c3349b5af023492458019eb7c8b5455affb3db8a37f5d94dc4d | encoding=utf-8-sig ---
from pathlib import Path
import re
import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

s=Path('Development/game-config.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
power=float(re.search(r'iroha:\{[^\n]*attackAngleExponent:([\d.]+)',s).group(1))
angle=np.linspace(0,90,901)
t=(angle-45)/45
cannon=100*(.5-.5*np.sign(t)*np.abs(t)**power)
plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':11})
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(10,5.5),dpi=150)
ax.plot(angle,cannon,color='#147db3',lw=2.8,label='Cannon')
ax.plot(angle,100-cannon,color='#d56727',lw=2.8,ls='--',label='Artillery')
ax.axvspan(70,90,color='#7d8a96',alpha=.10)
ax.axvline(70,color='#9ba5ad',lw=1,ls=':')
ax.text(80,50,'Faster change\nafter 70°',ha='center',va='center',color='#54616c',fontsize=10)
ax.scatter([45],[50],s=30,color='#54616c',zorder=5)
ax.annotate('50% / 50%',xy=(45,50),xytext=(28,65),arrowprops={'arrowstyle':'-','color':'#7d8a96'},fontsize=10)
ax.set(xlim=(0,90),ylim=(0,100),xlabel='Player angle from Iroha (0° = horizontal, 90° = vertical)',ylabel='Attack chance (%)',title='Iroha attack chance — current cubic curve')
ax.set_xticks([0,10,20,30,40,45,50,60,70,80,90])
ax.set_yticks(np.arange(0,101,10))
ax.grid(axis='y',color='#dce2e6',lw=.7)
ax.spines[['top','right']].set_visible(False)
ax.legend(loc='upper center',ncol=2,frameon=False,bbox_to_anchor=(.5,1))
fig.tight_layout()
fig.savefig('outputs/iroha-chance-curve/iroha-chance-curve.png',bbox_inches='tight')
assert cannon[0]==100 and cannon[450]==50 and cannon[-1]==0
print('Verified axis endpoints and 45-degree midpoint.')

--- SNAPSHOT FILE: tools/update_memory_activity_log.py | sha256=12114152ed20ce133023674bd8c5bf679173a00f22f36f5e5f775a18eb99f9bc | encoding=utf-8-sig ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild this chat's searchable, source-backed activity archive. Standard library only."""
import argparse
import base64
import collections
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import contextlib
import bisect

THREAD = '01a18d85-1c4b-7ee3-bcaa-39f9f31e2c4e'
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
LOG = ROOT / 'Memory Activity Log.txt'
INDEX = ROOT / 'Memory Activity Log.index.json'
PARTS = ROOT / 'Memory Activity Log Parts'
PART_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
CODE_SUFFIXES = {'.js', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.css', '.html', '.htm', '.json', '.py', '.ps1', '.sql', '.toml', '.yaml', '.yml', '.md', '.txt', '.svg'}
TOOL_TYPES = {'function_call', 'function_call_output', 'custom_tool_call', 'custom_tool_call_output'}
ACTIVITY_TYPES = {'CommandExecution', 'FileChange', 'McpToolCall', 'ImageView', 'WebSearch', 'Extension', 'SubAgentActivity'}


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def find_sources(home):
    found = []
    for base in [home / 'sessions', home / 'archived_sessions']:
        if not base.exists():
            continue
        for path in base.rglob('*.jsonl'):
            try:
                with path.open(encoding='utf-8') as stream:
                    first = json.loads(stream.readline())
                meta = first.get('payload', {})
                if first.get('type') == 'session_meta':
                    source = meta.get('source', {})
                    spawn = source.get('subagent', {}).get('thread_spawn', {}) if isinstance(source, dict) else {}
                    found.append((path, meta.get('id'), spawn.get('parent_thread_id'), spawn.get('agent_path')))
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                continue
    ids = {THREAD}
    while True:
        expanded = ids | {ident for _, ident, parent, _ in found if parent in ids}
        if expanded == ids:
            break
        ids = expanded
    return sorted((item for item in found if item[1] in ids), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))


def content_text(content):
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if not isinstance(content, list):
        return ''
    return '\n'.join(part.get('text', '') for part in content if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get('type', '').lower() in {'text', 'input_text', 'output_text'})


def safe_payload(payload):
    # Retain visible/tool content, not private runtime metadata or reasoning records.
    return {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key not in {'internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough', 'logprobs'}}


def ingest(db, sources):
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE records (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, timestamp TEXT, source TEXT, source_line INTEGER, scope TEXT, kind TEXT, role TEXT, phase TEXT, payload TEXT, text_hash TEXT)')
    reports = []
    seq = 0
    for path, ident, parent, agent_path in sources:
        report = {'path': str(path), 'session_id': ident, 'parent_thread_id': parent, 'agent_path': agent_path, 'bytes_at_read': path.stat().st_size,
                  'first_timestamp': None, 'last_timestamp': None, 'records': {}, 'parse_errors': [], 'source_truncation_markers': 0}
        counts = collections.Counter()
        source_hash = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open('rb') as stream:
            for number, raw in enumerate(stream, 1):
                source_hash.update(raw)
                try:
                    record = json.loads(raw)
                except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as error:
                    report['parse_errors'].append({'line': number, 'error': str(error)})
                    continue
                payload = record.get('payload', {})
                timestamp = record.get('timestamp', '')
                report['first_timestamp'] = report['first_timestamp'] or timestamp
                report['last_timestamp'] = timestamp
                category, kind = record.get('type'), None
                role = phase = ''
                data = None
                if category == 'response_item':
                    ptype = payload.get('type')
                    if ptype == 'message' and payload.get('role') in {'user', 'assistant'}:
                        phase = payload.get('phase', payload.get('channel', '')) or ''
                        if phase not in {'analysis', 'summary'}:
                            kind, role, data = 'MESSAGE', payload['role'], safe_payload(payload)
                    elif ptype in TOOL_TYPES:
                        kind, data = 'TOOL', safe_payload(payload)
                elif category == 'event_msg' and payload.get('type') == 'item_completed':
                    item = payload.get('item', {})
                    itype = item.get('type')
                    if itype in {'UserMessage', 'AgentMessage'}:
                        phase = item.get('phase', '') or ''
                        if phase not in {'analysis', 'summary'}:
                            kind, role, data = 'MESSAGE_MIRROR', 'user' if itype == 'UserMessage' else 'assistant', item
                    elif itype in ACTIVITY_TYPES:
                        kind, data = 'ACTIVITY', item
                if not kind:
                    continue
                text = content_text(data.get('content', [])) if role else ''
                encoded = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)
                report['source_truncation_markers'] += encoded.lower().count('output truncated') + encoded.lower().count('tokens truncated')
                counts[kind + (':' + role if role else '')] += 1
                seq += 1
                db.execute('INSERT INTO records VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                           (seq, timestamp, str(path), number, 'main' if ident == THREAD else (agent_path or ident), kind, role, phase, encoded,
                            digest((role + '\n' + phase + '\n' + text).encode('utf-8')) if role else None))
        report['sha256_at_read'] = source_hash.hexdigest()
        report['records'] = dict(counts)
        reports.append(report)
        db.commit()
    db.execute('CREATE INDEX record_message_hash ON records(text_hash,kind,timestamp)')
    return reports


class Writer:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.file = path.open('wb')
        self.line = 1
        self.sections = []

    def write(self, text):
        self.file.write(text.encode('utf-8'))
        self.line += text.count('\n')

    def section(self, title):
        self.sections.append({'title': title, 'line': self.line, 'byte': self.file.tell()})
        self.write('\n' + '=' * 78 + '\n' + title + '\n' + '=' * 78 + '\n')


def write_record(writer, row, number):
    _, timestamp, source, source_line, scope, kind, role, phase, encoded, _ = row
    payload = json.loads(encoded)
    entry = {'number': number, 'timestamp': timestamp, 'scope': scope, 'kind': kind, 'role': role, 'phase': phase,
             'line': writer.line, 'byte': writer.file.tell(), 'source': source, 'source_line': source_line,
             'source_id': payload.get('id')}
    writer.write(f'\n--- RECORD {number:06d} | {timestamp} | {scope} | {kind} {role} {phase} ---\nSOURCE: {source}:{source_line}\n')
    if role:
        text = content_text(payload.get('content', []))
        writer.write(text + '\n')
        entry['preview'] = text[:180].replace('\n', ' ')
        # Full content retains non-text attachments as recorded, without truncation.
        writer.write('\nFULL RECORDED MESSAGE (JSON; includes attachment blocks when present):\n')
        writer.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n')
    else:
        writer.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n')
        for key in ['input', 'command', 'arguments', 'aggregated_output', 'stdout', 'stderr', 'output']:
            value = payload.get(key)
            if isinstance(value, str) and value:
                writer.write(f'\nVERBATIM {key.upper()}:\n{value}\n')
        if payload.get('type') == 'FileChange':
            for name, change in payload.get('changes', {}).items():
                if not isinstance(change, dict):
                    continue
                for field in ['diff', 'content', 'patch']:
                    if isinstance(change.get(field), str):
                        writer.write(f'\nVERBATIM FILE CHANGE | {name} | {field}:\n{change[field]}\n')
    return entry


def export_git(writer):
    result = {}
    commands = [
        ('Git commit history and recorded line-level patches', ['git', 'log', '--all', '--reverse', '--root', '--format=fuller', '-p', '--no-ext-diff', '--', '.', ':(exclude)sprites/**', ':(exclude)Audio/**', ':(exclude)chat_log.txt', ':(exclude)Memory Activity Log*']),
        ('Current tracked working-tree patch (not an attribution of every line to the assistant)', ['git', 'diff', 'HEAD', '--no-ext-diff', '--', '.', ':(exclude)sprites/**', ':(exclude)Audio/**', ':(exclude)chat_log.txt', ':(exclude)Memory Activity Log*']),
        ('Current repository status', ['git', 'status', '--short'])]
    for title, command in commands:
        writer.section(title)
        writer.write('COMMAND: ' + ' '.join(command) + '\n')
        process = subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
        writer.write(process.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace'))
        error = process.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        if error:
            writer.write('\nGIT STDERR:\n' + error)
        result[title] = {'exit_code': process.returncode}
    return result


def snapshot(writer):
    writer.section('Current text/source files, verbatim baseline for future line-by-line comparisons')
    files = []
    ignored_dirs = {'.git', 'node_modules', '.codex', '.agents', '.aws', '.venv', '__pycache__', 'Audio', 'sprites', 'Memory Activity Log Parts'}
    for folder, dirs, names in os.walk(ROOT):
        dirs[:] = sorted(name for name in dirs if name not in ignored_dirs)
        for name in sorted(names):
            path = Path(folder) / name
            relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
            if name.startswith('Memory Activity Log') or name == 'chat_log.txt' or path.suffix.lower() not in CODE_SUFFIXES:
                continue
            raw = path.read_bytes()
            if b'\x00' in raw:
                continue
            try:
                text, encoding = raw.decode('utf-8-sig'), 'utf-8-sig'
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                text, encoding = raw.decode('cp1252', 'replace'), 'cp1252 (mixed historical files may contain legacy encoding)'
            item = {'path': relative, 'bytes': len(raw), 'sha256': digest(raw), 'encoding': encoding, 'line': writer.line, 'byte': writer.file.tell()}
            files.append(item)
            writer.write(f'\n--- SNAPSHOT FILE: {relative} | sha256={item["sha256"]} | encoding={encoding} ---\n')
            writer.write(text + ('\n' if not text.endswith('\n') else ''))
            if encoding != 'utf-8-sig':
                writer.write('RAW ORIGINAL BYTES (BASE64; exact mixed-encoding source):\n'+base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')+'\n')
    return files


def split_archive(source, index, target=PART_BYTES):
    """Lossless UTF-8 parts; prefer record boundaries and preserve original global offsets."""
    PARTS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    boundaries=sorted({0,*[item['byte'] for group in ('entries','sections','snapshot_files') for item in index[group]]})
    parts=[];full_hash=hashlib.sha256();offset=0;line=1
    with source.open('rb') as stream:
        while True:
            stream.seek(offset);block=stream.read(target)
            if not block:break
            if len(block)==target:
                end=offset+len(block)
                position=bisect.bisect_right(boundaries,end)-1
                boundary=boundaries[position] if position>=0 else 0
                if boundary>=offset+target//2:
                    block=block[:boundary-offset]
                else:
                    newline=block.rfind(b'\n')
                    if newline>=target//2:block=block[:newline+1]
                    else:
                        # A large recorded/base64 line may span parts. Never cut a UTF-8 codepoint.
                        while True:
                            try:block.decode('utf-8');break
                            except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
                                if error.start<len(block)-4:raise
                                block=block[:error.start]
            block.decode('utf-8')
            name=f'Memory Activity Log - {len(parts)+1:03d}.txt'
            output=PARTS/name;temporary=output.with_suffix('.building');temporary.write_bytes(block);os.replace(temporary,output)
            parts.append({'number':len(parts)+1,'file':output.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),'bytes':len(block),
                          'start_byte':offset,'end_byte':offset+len(block),'start_line':line,'sha256':digest(block)})
            full_hash.update(block);offset+=len(block);line+=block.count(b'\n')
    if offset!=index['bytes'] or full_hash.hexdigest()!=index['sha256']:
        raise RuntimeError('Split verification failed; original archive has not been replaced.')
    starts=[part['start_byte'] for part in parts]
    for group in ('entries','sections','snapshot_files'):
        for item in index[group]:
            part=parts[bisect.bisect_right(starts,item['byte'])-1]
            item.update(file=part['file'],part_byte=item['byte']-part['start_byte'],part_line=item['line']-part['start_line']+1)
    for part in parts:
        records=[entry for entry in index['entries'] if entry['file']==part['file']]
        part['first_record']=records[0]['number'] if records else None
        part['last_record']=records[-1]['number'] if records else None
        part['first_timestamp']=records[0]['timestamp'] if records else None
        part['last_timestamp']=records[-1]['timestamp'] if records else None
        part['sections']=[section['title'] for section in index['sections'] if section['file']==part['file']]
    index.update(format='ordered-lossless-parts-v1',parts=parts,part_target_bytes=target)
    guide=['MEMORY ACTIVITY LOG - START HERE / READING ORDER',
           'Original chat begins October 30, 2026 at 8:54 AM America/Vancouver.',
           'Generated UTC: '+index['generated_utc'],'',
           'The full archive is split into the numbered TXT files below (maximum 8 MiB each).',
           'Read them in ascending numeric order. Part 001 contains original coverage and limitations.',
           'Main conversation is chronological first; delegated records and supplemental evidence follow.',
           'These are verbatim consecutive segments, not summaries. A long record may continue into the next part.',
           'No headers are inserted into parts: concatenating their bytes exactly restores the full archive.',
           'Later user corrections override earlier records. Verify current code before relying on old implementations.',
           'Use Memory Activity Log.index.json to find records by preview, timestamp, source or section.',
           'Each entry now gives file, part_line and part_byte; original line/byte refer to the concatenated archive.',
           'Read only relevant ranges. Do not load the whole archive into context.',
           'Refresh: python tools/update_memory_activity_log.py','',
           f"Total: {len(parts)} parts; {index['bytes']:,} bytes; {index['records']:,} records.",
           'Verified concatenated SHA256: '+index['sha256'],'','READING ORDER']
    for part in parts:
        detail=f"{part['number']:03d}. {part['file']} ({part['bytes']/1024/1024:.2f} MiB)"
        if part['first_record'] is not None:
            detail+=f" | records starting here {part['first_record']:06d}-{part['last_record']:06d} | {part['first_timestamp']} to {part['last_timestamp']}"
        else:detail+=' | continuation / supplemental evidence'
        guide.append(detail)
        for title in part['sections']:guide.append('     Section: '+title)
    guide.extend(['','SECTION LOCATOR'])
    guide.extend(f"{section['title']}: {section['file']} line {section['part_line']}" for section in index['sections'])
    return '\n'.join(guide)+'\n'


def build(home, final_response=None):
    sources = find_sources(home)
    if not any(ident == THREAD for _, ident, _, _ in sources):
        raise SystemExit('The original chat archives were not found; no log was overwritten.')
    generated = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    temp = LOG.with_suffix('.building')
    with contextlib.closing(sqlite3.connect(':memory:')) as db:
        reports = ingest(db, sources)
        counts = dict(db.execute('SELECT kind || CASE WHEN role != "" THEN ":" || role ELSE "" END, count(*) FROM records GROUP BY kind,role'))
        writer = Writer(temp)
        writer.write('MEMORY ACTIVITY LOG\nOriginal chat: Friday, October 30, 2026 at 8:54 AM America/Vancouver\n')
        writer.write('Opening request: "For the first step, could you create a txt file that logs all prompts, responses, changes and chat logs there"\n')
        writer.write(f'Thread: {THREAD}\nGenerated UTC: {generated}\nCompanion searchable index: Memory Activity Log.index.json\nRefresh: python tools/update_memory_activity_log.py\n\n')
        writer.write('READ THIS FIRST\nThis is a source-backed archive, not a condensed replacement for the conversation. It preserves available user and visible assistant messages, questions, tool calls/results, recorded file-change patches, and code-writing commands. Supporting tool-event mirrors are deliberately retained. Original source paths/line numbers are provided for auditing.\n')
        writer.write('Coverage: starts with the exact October 30 opening request and continues through the last available records listed below. A refresh captures subsequent turns. Internal reasoning, system/developer instructions, and private runtime configuration are not conversation content and are not exported. No unavailable or already-truncated historical content is invented. Original recordings may already contain truncation or interrupted actions; those limitations remain visible.\n')
        writer.write('Code-history limits: recorded patches and write commands are preserved verbatim, with Git history/current diff and a current source snapshot as additional evidence. Shell-based writes do not always record intermediate before/after file contents. Changes made outside the recorded tools, missing archive segments, and unrecorded intermediate file versions cannot be guaranteed recoverable. Git/current snapshots may also contain user edits; they are labelled evidence, not claimed assistant authorship. Binary project assets are outside the text/source snapshot, while recorded attachment/tool blocks are preserved as present in the archives.\n')
        writer.write('Memory use: consult this header, the index and the latest relevant records, then search this file for the topic/file/record number. Later user corrections supersede older instructions; old designs remain here as historical records. Never load the whole archive into a context window.\n\n')
        writer.write('SOURCE COVERAGE\n' + json.dumps(reports, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\nRECORD COUNTS\n' + json.dumps(counts, indent=2) + '\n')
        writer.section('Chronological main conversation and tool/code activity')
        entries = []
        skipped_mirrors = 0
        for row in db.execute('SELECT * FROM records WHERE scope="main" ORDER BY timestamp,seq'):
            if row[5] == 'MESSAGE_MIRROR':
                # Suppress presentation mirrors only when matching visible text/phase exists nearby.
                timestamp = dt.datetime.fromisoformat(row[1].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
                nearby = db.execute('SELECT timestamp FROM records WHERE text_hash=? AND kind="MESSAGE" AND scope="main"', (row[9],)).fetchall()
                if any(abs((timestamp-dt.datetime.fromisoformat(value[0].replace('Z','+00:00'))).total_seconds()) < 5 for value in nearby):
                    skipped_mirrors += 1
                    continue
            entries.append(write_record(writer, row, len(entries) + 1))
        writer.section('Delegated work: associated child-agent visible messages and tool/code records')
        for row in db.execute('SELECT * FROM records WHERE scope!="main" ORDER BY timestamp,seq'):
            # Child task instructions are labelled delegated activity, not additional user prompts.
            entries.append(write_record(writer, row, len(entries) + 1))
        if final_response:
            writer.section('Final response prepared for this archive-generation turn')
            writer.write(final_response + '\n')
        writer.section('Existing chat_log.txt preserved verbatim as supplemental historical evidence')
        old = ROOT / 'chat_log.txt'
        raw = old.read_bytes() if old.exists() else b''
        try:
            oldtext = raw.decode('utf-8-sig')
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            oldtext = raw.decode('cp1252', 'replace')
        writer.write(oldtext + '\n')
        writer.write('RAW ORIGINAL chat_log.txt BYTES (BASE64; exact encoding preserved):\n'+base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')+'\n')
        old_hash = digest(raw)
        git = export_git(writer)
        files = snapshot(writer)
        writer.section('Archive verification and refresh instructions')
        writer.write(f'Exported records: {len(entries)}\nPresentation mirrors suppressed (matching canonical message within 5 seconds): {skipped_mirrors}\nSource snapshot files: {len(files)}\nOriginal chat_log.txt SHA256: {old_hash}\n')
        writer.write('Refresh after future work: python tools/update_memory_activity_log.py\nThe source archives are read-only; this tool only rebuilds the workspace archive and its index. Regeneration captures later records, including previous refresh results. Source-truncated text is never silently filled in.\n')
        sections = writer.sections
        writer.file.close()
        archive_hash = hashlib.sha256()
        with temp.open('rb') as stream:
            while block := stream.read(1024 * 1024):
                archive_hash.update(block)
        index = {'thread_id': THREAD, 'generated_utc': generated, 'log': LOG.name, 'bytes': temp.stat().st_size, 'sha256': archive_hash.hexdigest(),
                 'records': len(entries), 'source_counts': counts, 'presentation_mirrors_suppressed': skipped_mirrors, 'sources': reports,
                 'sections': sections, 'entries': entries, 'snapshot_files': files, 'git': git, 'chat_log_sha256': old_hash}
        guide=split_archive(temp,index)
        INDEX.write_text(json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
        LOG.write_text(guide,encoding='utf-8')
        temp.unlink()
        current={part['file'] for part in index['parts']}
        for old in PARTS.glob('Memory Activity Log - [0-9][0-9][0-9].txt'):
            if old.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix() not in current:old.unlink()
        db.close()
        print(json.dumps({'log': str(LOG), 'bytes': index['bytes'], 'parts':len(index['parts']), 'records': len(entries), 'snapshot_files': len(files), 'sources': len(reports),
                          'parse_errors': sum(len(r['parse_errors']) for r in reports), 'sha256': index['sha256']}, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--codex-home', type=Path, default=Path.home()/'.codex')
    parser.add_argument('--final-response', help='Exact prepared visible final response, captured before it is sent.')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    build(args.codex_home, args.final_response)

import importlib.util
import uuid
import contextlib
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
import unittest
import hashlib
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('activity',Path(__file__).parents[1]/'update_memory_activity_log.py')
activity=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(activity)
@contextlib.contextmanager
def fixture_directory():
    base=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    folder=base/('split-fixture-'+uuid.uuid4().hex)
    folder.mkdir()
    try:yield folder
    finally:
        assert folder.resolve().parent==base and folder.name.startswith('split-fixture-')
        shutil.rmtree(folder)
class SplitTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_lossless_unicode_and_record_locations(self):
        original_root,original_parts=activity.ROOT,activity.PARTS
        try:
            with fixture_directory() as folder:
                activity.ROOT=Path(folder);activity.PARTS=Path(folder)/'parts'
                text='header\n'+('record alpha\n'*8)+('long '+chr(0x1f31f)*400)+'\nlast record\n'
                raw=text.encode();source=Path(folder)/'source';source.write_bytes(raw)
                offset=raw.index(b'last record')
                index={'generated_utc':'test','bytes':len(raw),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),'records':1,
                       'sections':[],'snapshot_files':[],'entries':[{'number':1,'byte':offset,'line':raw[:offset].count(b'\n')+1,'timestamp':'test'}]}
                activity.split_archive(source,index,target=128)
                chunks=[(activity.ROOT/p['file']).read_bytes() for p in index['parts']]
                self.assertEqual(b''.join(chunks),raw)
                self.assertTrue(all(len(chunk)<=128 for chunk in chunks))
                for chunk in chunks:chunk.decode('utf-8')
                entry=index['entries'][0];part=(activity.ROOT/entry['file']).read_bytes()
                self.assertTrue(part[entry['part_byte']:].startswith(b'last record'))
                self.assertEqual(part[:entry['part_byte']].count(b'\n')+1,entry['part_line'])
        finally:activity.ROOT,activity.PARTS=original_root,original_parts
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()
